| AK-1 | The canonical repository governs every workflow. Every Stage 2 record carries the authority order; every difference is resolved by adapting the reference |
| AK-2 | P0–P6 stay unchanged. No P0–P6 edit; a conflict is recorded as OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED and never enacted |
| AK-3 | The R1 structure stays. The IA and the page inventory are built from R1 — the Admin task-first, the Owner decision-first, the Project Journey and the role-specific navigation —, and the non-loss map is checked against SCR-01–SCR-60 |
| AK-4 | The reference for visuals and presentation. Its runtime files are used unchanged; a visual deviation goes only through a recorded project override layer |
| AK-5 | None of the reference's demonstration material. The prototype is written new from the canonical documents; a dedicated checklist proves that no route, workflow, role, fixture, logic or capability outside V1 is imported |
| AK-6 | Every journey is traced. A per-step trace matrix: action → command (AX, SF-CMD) → transition (WF, BR, CM) → permission (capability; AZ, CS, PJ, OD) → message (MSG) → screen (SCR) → signal or data (QS, CALC). A step without a canonical source is removed |
| AK-7 | The reference adapts. A deviation register continues GR-01–GR-23; business semantics never change |
| AK-8 | Synthetic data and marked simulation. The repository's synthetic set — the companies ARJ, BTN and CKP; Laras (Owner), Dimas (Admin) and Bayu (warehouse) —, a simulation register and a prototype marker (SS-08) |

| S2-J03 | The Admin's *Pekerjaan* → Project Journey → "Buat invoice" → the composer with "DRAF — belum terbit" → issue with the one-step confirmation | Admin; desktop, and 390 px as below | AN-3, AN-4 | ADMIN_FLOW J-FIN-01 (SCR-39; AX-16; `invoice.issue`, with `finance.view`) and IP-08; CONCURRENCY_IDEMPOTENCY NM-03; VD-07; DC-1–DC-12; PL-1–PL-3; C1A-1–C1A-3 |
| S2-J04 | Buat Pembelian with lines and charges, one line linked to a project | Admin; desktop | AN-5 | ADMIN_FLOW J-PUR-01, J-PUR-02 and IP-13; WORKFLOWS WF-PUR-01 and WF-PUR-02 |
| S2-J05 | Barang Masuk with scanning — three outcomes per line, the reservation offer, the summary, the Nota Terima Barang | The warehouse persona; phone at 390 and 320 px | AN-6, never as a behavioural source | ADMIN_FLOW J-INV-01 and IP-19; WORKFLOWS WF-INV-01 and AX-04; CONCURRENCY_IDEMPOTENCY HO-01; S2-R01–S2-R09 |
| S2-J06 | Recording a partial payment and applying it — new and narrow | Admin; desktop | — | ADMIN_FLOW J-FIN-03; WORKFLOWS WF-FIN-03, AX-18, AX-19 (the application invariants), SF-APPLY and L-29; BUSINESS_RULES BR-FIN-03, BR-FIN-04, BR-FIN-05, BR-FIN-07 and the numeric annex items 1–3; `payment.record`, with `finance.view`, which covers same-command applications — a later, separate application is `payment.apply`, outside this journey |

| Item | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| S2-J04 feeds S2-J05 | The project-linked purchase line of S2-J04 is the line received in S2-J05 |
| S2-J03's mobile validation | Without a second application: the same screens, responsive, with R1's phone navigation. At 390 px *Pekerjaan* and the Project Journey are fully interactive — open a work group, open a project, the disclosures, *Sekarang · Berikutnya · Hambatan*, the next action. Invoice issue is `[desk]` (J-FIN-01): on the phone that step shows as the canonical rules say, confirmed in S2-1's trace matrix; no new phone version is made |
| S2-J06 — in | One payment fact — date, amount above zero, method, the company's bank account, the statement line it matches, reference and proof — with same-command applications (AX-18, through SF-APPLY) to issued invoices of the same client and company (BR-FIN-03; BR-FIN-07); each application at most the invoice's outstanding amount (BR-FIN-04) and their sum at most the payment (BR-FIN-03), the rest staying *Kredit Pelanggan* (BR-FIN-05); the partial example of the numeric annex item 1 — invoice Rp1.000.000, paid Rp300.000, outstanding Rp700.000; the simulated rejections of an application above the outstanding amount and of applications above the payment; a missing proof needing a reason, the payment flagged (L-29) |
| S2-J06 — out, documented only | Fee or tax deductions, refund, reallocation, contra, the Kuitansi link, one statement line for several clients and write-off |

**Reservation checks S2-R01–S2-R09** (SD-06). The canonical rules already say that receiving only offers an optional same-action reservation; S2-J05 and its trace matrix must pass all of these:

| ID | Check | Canonical sources | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| S2-R01 | Not automatic | AX-04 ("optional same-action reservation"); WF-INV-01 ("offer"); J-INV-01 | The offer is not preselected; without an explicit choice no reservation is made |
| S2-R02 | Confirmed demand only | BR-RSV-01; BR-PRJ-06 — quotation approval, or an explicit confirmation decision with the client's order evidence; BR-QUO-03 and V1_SCOPE "Reservation and usable stock" — a quotation alone reserves nothing; AX-01 — confirmed WAREHOUSE demand only | A project whose quotation is SENT and not APPROVED gets no offer |
| S2-R03 | Project-linked lines only | WF-INV-01 ("for a project-linked line"); J-INV-01; WF-PUR-02 — without a project, received goods become free pool stock | A line without a project gets no offer |
| S2-R04 | Usable quantity only | BR-RSV-01; L-13's three outcomes — *Rusak diterima* goes to UNUSABLE, *Ditolak* creates no stock event; J-INV-01 ("a quantity of the usable units") | No offer for a damaged-accepted or rejected part |
| S2-R05 | Bounded | BR-RSV-01 (AVAILABLE ≥ 0); AX-01 | The offered quantity is at most the lesser of the usable quantity received and the line's confirmed demand not yet reserved |
| S2-R06 | The permission | PERMISSIONS_MATRIX `stock.receive`: the same-action reservation offer needs `stock.reserve` | A persona without `stock.reserve` receives normally and gets no offer |
| S2-R07 | One command | AX-04; J-INV-01 ("sent with the receipt as one command"); IP-19 | Receipt and reservation give one answer per line |
| S2-R08 | When skipped | QS-03 — received goods for a project awaiting reservation | A skipped offer appears in that signal |
| S2-R09 | Never before receipt, never a physical movement | BR-RSV-02; BR-RSV-03 | Goods not yet received are never reserved, and a reservation moves no stock |

**Visual checks SV-01–SV-06** (SD-07; run in S2-2 on every screen of S2-J01–S2-J06):

| ID | Check |
| --- | --- |
| SV-01 | Rendered side by side with the reference at the same viewport; the rendered styles — font, size, colour, border, radius, shadow, height — measured against the reference's tokens; only registered deviations |
| SV-02 | Responsive: desktop 1440×900 and 1366×768; phone 390×844 and 320 px wide; no horizontal scroll except data tables |
| SV-03 | Typography and breathing room: the type scale measured per viewport and judged with AICWDF §18.2's checklist; for the Owner's preview, GR-16 in two variants — the reference's standard, about 12.9 px at 1,366 px, and a 14 px floor —, and the Owner chooses |
| SV-04 | Motion: durations and easing audited against the reference's tokens and VP-8; reduced motion verified |
| SV-05 | Accessibility, WCAG 2.2 AA at design level: text contrast at least 4.5:1 and non-text at least 3:1, measured on the render; focus visible and not obscured; targets of 24, 44 and 48 px; a keyboard path through every journey; text spacing and reflow; status messages; information never by colour alone |
| SV-06 | Evidence: render files stored in the private prototype repository; MULTIPLECORP records their hashes only, no binary (P-4) |

**Safety rules SS-01–SS-08** (SD-07; binding on every run from S2-0 — SS-08 from the run that builds journey screens; S2-0's empty shell carries the marker only):

| ID | Rule |
| --- | --- |
| SS-01 | Synthetic data only: the repository's set — the fictitious companies ARJ, BTN and CKP, "Contoh" names for clients and suppliers, and AK-8's personas |
| SS-02 | No real credential: no real e-mail address, password or TOTP secret; typed codes are never stored |
| SS-03 | No live OAuth: Google is simulated — no OAuth client, no call to Google (GAP-042 stays in P9) |
| SS-04 | A read-only server: Node.js's built-in `http` module, the static files of the prototype's web root only, no write endpoint, safe against path traversal |
| SS-05 | No outbound request: no CDN, internet font or analytics — shown by the network log when rendering |
| SS-06 | Network: by default only 127.0.0.1. LAN access only during a session the Owner moderates, on a network with the Windows "Private" profile, never on public Wi-Fi, with any firewall allowance for the Private profile only. No port forwarding, tunnel or public hosting; the repository stays private; the server is stopped after use |
| SS-07 | No backend, generic CRUD or storage of any kind, browser storage included |
| SS-08 | Simulation is marked: a permanent "PROTOTIPE · data contoh" badge in the shell, outside OC-06's copy budget; a bilingual panel "Tentang prototipe ini" opened from it; a simulation register separating simulated behaviour — authentication, TOTP and Google; permission checks; numbering; idempotency and `command_id`; stock and finance posting; PDF; the scanner — from design-faithful behaviour — the flow order, screens, states, confirmations and messages; a "Simulasi" chip only where something could be mistaken for a real system effect, such as an issued document number; the moderator's script explains this at the start of a session. The prototype never presents itself as a working system |

**Planning interpretations SP-1–SP-10** (Level 1; recorded with DIR-062):

| ID | Interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| SP-1 The vendored set | Copied byte-identical, under SD-04's restrictions: the five stylesheets (base, components, motion, patterns, tokens); six runtime scripts (composer, docs, guide, interactions, patterns, router); and Inter's four files from the reference's public package — `InterVariable.woff2`, `InterVariable-Italic.woff2`, `LICENSE.txt` and `README.md`; Appendix F lists the 15 with their SHA-256. This narrows the blueprint's "Layer A copied byte-identical": the reference's manifest puts `icons.js` and `template/page-skeleton.html` in its Layer A, and neither is copied. Not copied: `assets/js/icons.js` — its sprite draws the Google "G" (`i-google`) and the MultipleCorp mark (`i-logo-mark`), which SD-04 keeps out, GR-18 already setting Lucide, styled to the reference's strokes, as the icon direction, and S2-1 deciding the prototype's icon source; `template/page-skeleton.html` — a template, not runtime, carrying the reference's demonstration content (its persona in the account block, its demonstration links and scripts and a brand-mark favicon), the S2-0 shell following its anatomy, written new (Appendix D); `assets/js/project-config.js` — the reference's MULTIPLECORP demonstration configuration: its routes, modules, fixtures, personas and navigation trees, and its preview hooks kept in session storage (GD-04; AK-5; the GA1 reconciliation's parts g) and h)); the other templates, the documents, examples, tools and `serve.cmd` — not runtime, read from a verified clone when a later run needs them; anything of the reference's private lab or its 239 demonstration files — never. The six scripts stay inert in S2-0, no page loading them: several depend on the runtime API of `project-config.js` (`MCProject`, `MCRoutes`, `MCTime`, `MCBrand`) or on the sprite of `icons.js`, some use browser storage or a same-origin fetch, and several stylesheet rules depend on the `mc-js` flag that `icons.js` sets; VENDOR.md inventories this, and S2-1 decides which scripts load and how. The example-page blocks of the stylesheets (`.pur-*`, `.atq-*`, `.prj-*`, and the original pages' sections) stay unused |
| SP-2 The configuration follows S2-1 | The blueprint's "project configuration from the template" is narrowed in S2-0 to recording, in the prototype's README, the project values MULTIPLECORP already fixes: the name MultipleCorp; Bahasa Indonesia by default with English (CAP-14); the Asia/Jakarta business time zone (BR-DT-06); the synthetic set (GP-4) — the configuration's routes, navigation and roles being S2-1's IA (AK-3). S2-1 proposes how the reference's runtime API is provided without its demonstration configuration and without browser storage; since SD-04 covers byte-identical copies only, whether a configuration "written new from the template" covers a modified copy of `project-config.js` — the reference's own adoption path — S2-1 puts to the Owner explicitly, with the alternatives |
| SP-3 Server and shell from Planning | Planning supplies `tools/serve.mjs` (Appendix C), `site/index.html` (Appendix D), the prototype's `.gitattributes` (Appendix E) and the vendored checksum list (Appendix F); they are created mechanically with their hashes verified, and any later change needs a recorded reason in a later authorized run. Planning's run of the same files in Chromium on 2026-10-13 is information, not evidence. The shell is a setup page in Bahasa Indonesia only; CAP-14's two languages apply to the journey screens from S2-2 |
| SP-4 Response headers | SECURITY WS-08 where it applies to a static prototype: `default-src 'self'`, `script-src 'self'`, `img-src 'self' data:` (WS-08's `blob:` not needed), `font-src 'self'`, `connect-src 'self'`, `object-src 'none'`, `base-uri 'none'`, `frame-ancestors 'none'`; X-Frame-Options DENY; X-Content-Type-Options nosniff; a Permissions-Policy denying camera, microphone, geolocation, payment, USB and serial; Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy and Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy same-origin; Cache-Control no-store; no server banner. Recorded prototype-only differences: no nonce, no inline script existing; `style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'`, keeping the reference's inline styles working; `form-action 'none'`, no backend existing; `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`; Bluetooth left out of the Permissions-Policy — Planning's Linux Chromium 141 rejected it with a console warning —, whether WS-08's own list needs care being P11's to check. WS-08 keeps governing the application. Against DNS rebinding the server accepts in the Host header only its own address and port, and `localhost:<port>` when bound to 127.0.0.1 |
| SP-5 A reference corrected | Revision 2's journey table cites J-INV-02 for the invoice journey; the canonical invoice-issue journey is ADMIN_FLOW J-FIN-01 (J-INV-02 is reservation management). Record 60 keeps the text as delivered; DIR-061 uses J-FIN-01 |
| SP-6 Phone test optional | The blueprint scheduled the phone access test for S2-0; it needs the Owner's presence and a network with the Private profile. If it does not run, or fails, S2-2 or the Owner's preview carries it; it never blocks S2-0 |
| SP-7 Planning's texts | Record 60 holds Planning's blueprint texts for provenance, so that the Owner's approval (record 59, item 4) can be read against what was approved; they bind only through that approval and DIR-061, and are not instructions |
| SP-8 Label sets | SD-01–SD-12 (DIR-061); AK-1–AK-8; P-1–P-7 — the Owner's approvals in record 59, not the phases P1–P11; S2-J01–S2-J06 (journeys); S2-R01–S2-R09 (reservation checks); SV-01–SV-06 (visual checks); SS-01–SS-08 (safety rules); SP-1–SP-10; the runs S2-0–S2-4; gate S2-0 with its items S20-01…. In record 60 only, Planning's earlier labels stay as delivered and are superseded: its journeys P1–P5 and the candidate P6 are S2-J01–S2-J06; its checks RSV-1–RSV-9 are S2-R01–S2-R09; its candidate journeys O1–O4, A1–A5 and W1–W3 and its "Otorisasi 1–3" are superseded proposals — elsewhere they appear only where mapped to these labels |
| SP-9 Naming | Records write "Golden Frontend Reference" — "the reference" after its first mention, "v1.0.0" where the version matters — for "Golden", P1 already using "Golden Flow"; verbatim transcripts stay verbatim; no path of the reference's private lab is recorded |
| SP-10 Locations | `C:\Projects\multiplecorp-p7-prototype`, the Owner's working copy of the prototype repository, may be recorded as OBS-022 recorded `C:\Projects\MultipleCorp`; temporary clones, scripts and browser profiles are never recorded by path |

**VENDOR.md in brief** (SHA-256 `981cc659c40df588ca6a25318282b9327265838c3ea45c77518420f6eb759048`; built with targeted searches, not whole-file reads):

| Area | Finding |
| --- | --- |
| Scripts loaded in S2-0 | None: the shell has no `<script>` element, and the six vendored scripts stay inert |
| The runtime API of the non-vendored `project-config.js` | Needed by five scripts — `MCProject`, `MC_PROJECT`, `MCRoutes`, `MCTime` and `MCBrand`, and router.js's `MC_PROJECT_PATCH`; interactions.js most, with 30 occurrences of `MCProject`; docs.js needs none of it |
| The sprite of the non-vendored `icons.js` | Needed by composer.js, guide.js, interactions.js and patterns.js through their `icon()` helpers; one `i-logo-mark` reference, in interactions.js's phone and tablet drawer; no `i-google` reference |
| Dependencies between the scripts | composer.js on docs.js, guide.js, patterns.js and router.js; guide.js on interactions.js; patterns.js on interactions.js and router.js; router.js on interactions.js and patterns.js; interactions.js on guide.js, patterns.js and router.js; docs.js on none |
| Browser storage | `localStorage`: `mc.guide.level`, `mc.guide.recent` and `mc.guide.tour` (guide.js), `mc-shell-sidebar` (interactions.js); `sessionStorage`: `mc:prev` and `mc:range` (interactions.js), `mc:company` (patterns.js), `mc:router-fallback` and `mc:prev` (router.js) — each outside SS-07 if the script were loaded unchanged |
| Network | One same-origin `fetch` of in-app pages (router.js) and one `new Image()` for a media address a page passes in (patterns.js); no XMLHttpRequest, WebSocket, EventSource, beacon, dynamic import or service worker |
| Demonstration content | Only in interactions.js: in its Login (01) section a generic sample sign-in e-mail placeholder in each language and role-keyed copy; in its Motion Study (04) section and command palette, sample records — company names, Rupiah amounts, document numbers, a company code and sample project, client, goods and warehouse names; no persona name. Counted in VENDOR.md, never quoted |
| Stylesheets | All five loaded by the shell; no `@import`; outside comments only `../fonts/inter/…` and `data:` SVG images — two masks in components.css, one background in patterns.css; the example-page blocks — components.css's Login, Owner Dashboard, Create Purchase and Motion Study sections with their phone layouts and section 31, patterns.css's `atq-` and `prj-` blocks, motion.css's page sections, a login-scoped token override in tokens.css — never used; 38 lines of components.css depend on the `mc-js` flag, inactive without `icons.js` |
| Fonts | base.css declares Inter normal and italic and an `Inter Fallback` family on local Segoe UI faces; the shell loads `InterVariable.woff2` only |

**Checks of §11.** The server ran from the working copy on 127.0.0.1, port 8780 (free), and was started and stopped within each block of checks; it was never left running.

*§11.1 Server* (`curl --path-as-is`, so that the client does not normalize the paths):

| Check | Result | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |

| Refused arguments | PASS | `--host 0.0.0.0`, `--host ::`, `--host localhost`, `--host 8.8.8.8`, `--host 127.0.0.2`, `--port 80`, `--port abc` and an unknown argument each exit with code 2 and a message, without listening |
| Methods | PASS | GET / → 200 `text/html; charset=utf-8`; HEAD / → 200 with no body; POST, PUT, DELETE and OPTIONS → 405 with `Allow: GET, HEAD` |
| Headers (SP-4) | PASS | Every response checked — 200, 301, 400, 404 and 405 — carries the exact Content-Security-Policy, X-Content-Type-Options, X-Frame-Options, Referrer-Policy, Permissions-Policy, Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy, Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy and Cache-Control values, with no `Server` or `X-Powered-By` header |
| Refusals | PASS | 404 for `/../tools/serve.mjs`, `/%2e%2e/tools/serve.mjs`, `/..%2ftools%2fserve.mjs`, `/./index.html`, `/.gitattributes`, `/vendor/golden-frontend-reference-v1.0.0/assets/fonts/inter/README.md`, `/CON` and `/nonexistent.html`; 400 for `/index.html%00.txt`, `/%5c..%5ctools%5cserve.mjs`, `/%` and a request with `Host: example.invalid`; `Host: localhost:8780` accepted |
| Directory | PASS | `/vendor` → 301 to `/vendor/`, which answers 404 — no listing |
| Font | PASS | `/vendor/golden-frontend-reference-v1.0.0/assets/fonts/inter/InterVariable.woff2` → 200 `font/woff2`, its body byte-identical to the file |
| Second start | PASS | A second start on the same port exits with code 2: "port 8780 is already in use" |
| Stop | PASS | After stopping, the port no longer listens and no server process is left |

*§11.2 Shell.* Google Chrome 155.0.8059.39 in headless mode (`--headless=new`) with a fresh temporary profile outside both repositories, deleted afterwards, and the flags of §11.2; driven over the DevTools protocol, through a pipe, by a script on the installed Node.js's built-in modules, kept outside both repositories — nothing installed. ## Stage 2 — S2-1 inputs

DIR-062 §13.6 — what S2-1 must settle, non-normative; nothing of S2-1 starts here (SD-12).

| Input | What S2-1 must settle |
| --- | --- |
| The runtime API (SP-2) | Five of the six vendored scripts expect the runtime API of the reference's `project-config.js` — `MCProject`, `MC_PROJECT`, `MCRoutes`, `MCTime` and `MCBrand`, and router.js's `MC_PROJECT_PATCH` —, a file not vendored because it carries the reference's demonstration configuration. S2-1 proposes how that API is provided without the demonstration configuration and without browser storage. If the proposal needs a modified copy of `project-config.js` — the reference's own adoption path, its ROUTES, MODULES and FIXTURES replaced —, S2-1 puts that question to the Owner explicitly, with the alternatives, because SD-04 covers byte-identical copies only |
| The icon source (GR-18; SP-1) | `icons.js` and its sprite are not vendored, while four scripts build `<use>` references to `#i-` symbols and 38 lines of components.css wait for the `mc-js` flag that `icons.js` sets. S2-1 decides the prototype's icon source — Lucide styled to the reference's strokes (GR-18) — and how the flag is set without the reference's sprite |
| Scripts and browser storage (SS-07; GP-7) | Which vendored scripts load, and in which order, and how browser storage stays out: the keys VENDOR.md lists — `mc.guide.level`, `mc.guide.recent`, `mc.guide.tour`, `mc-shell-sidebar`, `mc:prev`, `mc:range`, `mc:company` and `mc:router-fallback` — would break SS-07 if their scripts were loaded unchanged; router.js's same-origin page fetch and its persistent shell need a decision too |
| Verification tooling for SD-07 | Found in S2-0: Google Chrome 155.0.8059.39 and Microsoft Edge 155.0.4283.45 installed; Node.js v24.18.0 with a global `WebSocket`. S2-0 measured the shell through Chrome's DevTools protocol over a pipe, from Node.js built-ins, with no package. S2-1 states whether SV-01–SV-06 can run the same way and, if a package would be needed, the installation to ask the Owner for |
| The marker and the panel (SS-08) | The S2-0 shell shows the "PROTOTIPE · data contoh" pill in its page head; S2-1 places the permanent badge in the shell, outside OC-06's copy budget, and designs the bilingual panel "Tentang prototipe ini" opened from it, with the simulation register and the "Simulasi" chip |
| The screen list (SD-01) | S2-1 locks the screen list of S2-J01–S2-J06, the out-of-scope page included, for the Owner's approval before S2-2 |
| The phone test | NOT RUN in S2-0 — the network profile Public, by the Owner's choice; S2-2 or the Owner's preview carries it, on a trusted network with the Private profile and with the Owner present (SP-6; SS-06) |
| The simulated Google sign-in (S2-J01) | SD-04 keeps the reference's drawn Google "G" out, SS-03 and SS-05 rule out OAuth and outbound requests, and GR-13 leaves the production button to P11 under Google's branding guidelines; VC1's anchor used a neutral "G". S2-1 proposes how the prototype draws the simulated Google button — a neutral placeholder, or another option within the brand rules — for the Owner to decide |
| Blueprint content still in force | Content of revision 2, and of the first blueprint it revises, that DIR-061's §2 does not restate stays an approved-in-principle input — for example both languages with no missing string, a zero-context check in every run, draft routes as input for GAP-035, and the mapping verified again against official documentation. S2-1 lists the items it relies on, with their place in the sixtieth source record, so that the S2-1 authorization can restate them |
| Behaviour sources for S2-J02 | INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE §9 and D-IA-07, among S2-J02's principal sources, are APPROVED under APPR-008 and under replacement, D-IA-01–D-IA-17 SUPERSEDED-PENDING; S2-1's trace matrix states which of their elements — for example the switch between Gabungan and one company — remain behaviour sources under AK-3 and R1's Beranda (OC-05) |

These files lie outside S2-0's list (S20-11): a narrow continuity record for a later authorized run |

Planning's independent verification (SD-01) needs read access to it, which the Owner arranges — for example a read-only collaborator, or the Owner running the checks; S2-0 changed no GitHub setting |

The example-page blocks of the stylesheets stay unused — components.css's page sections 01–04 with their phone layouts and section 31, patterns.css's `atq-` and `prj-` blocks, motion.css's page sections and a login-scoped override in tokens.css. ## Stage 2 — S2-0 report

DIR-062 §17 — the English equivalent of the report given to the Owner in Bahasa Indonesia at the stop before S2-1.

1. **The decisions recorded.** DIR-061 opens Stage 2 under one run per authorization (SD-01); makes AK-1–AK-8 binding, with logout following MSG-35 and the reference's filter toolbar as presentation only (SD-02); sets the private prototype repository as the medium, with its permission (SD-03, SD-04); fixes the six journeys S2-J01–S2-J06, with S2-J03's validation at 390 px and S2-J06's scope (SD-05); allows a reservation only for approved, confirmed demand, checked by S2-R01–S2-R09 (SD-06); sets the visual checks SV-01–SV-06 and the safety rules SS-01–SS-08 (SD-07); makes the Owner's visual preview a formal gate (SD-08); keeps three roles with K2's fallback (SD-09); places the drafts under `docs/07-ux-design/stage2/` (SD-10); keeps the rest unchanged (SD-11); and authorizes S2-0 only (SD-12). They are in source records 59–61, in DECISION_LOG (DIR-061, DIR-062, OBS-023, TECH-031), DECISION_INDEX and SOURCE_OF_TRUTH, and in [Stage 2 — Owner decisions (DIR-061)](#stage-2--owner-decisions-dir-061).
2. **The MULTIPLECORP baseline (OBS-023).** Every item of §5 passed; commit 8 `e9538a569b5b7f8d46fbb6254394c010d56bd6c4` and its branch push are recorded literally; `main` stays at `a526daf47444d12b4ae5c51e1ae14c9dfe3f4978`.
3. **The prototype repository.** Private — 404 without credentials — and empty before the setup; its first commit `503e83bd3ed720f2af1d7c5eb4bc0f84500b9edb` on `main`, with no parent and under the Owner's identity, pushed normally and verified on the live remote — HEAD → `main` at that commit, no other ref —, still 404 without credentials afterwards. Its 25 files: `.gitattributes`, README.md, VENDOR.md, `tools/serve.mjs`, `site/index.html`, the vendored checksum list, the reference's 15 files and four screenshots ([prototype setup](#stage-2--s2-0-prototype-setup)).
4. **The reference.** Its tag `v1.0.0` — tag object `9d46af300949b2dd4b2f3d443652d78c38067deb`, commit `14497d997ee7994dfa9153d41b8cea636578e24c` —, its 41 scope and 55 public checksums OK with the list digests `39fa879d…eca4` and `5f395e5c…8b67`, frozen and Owner-approved on 2026-10-12. Vendored byte-identical: five stylesheets, six runtime scripts and Inter's four files, list digest `81b734a4fbf8277f3e79f70016d65dc18dfa68843078ff4c388898a77f2cbb62`. Not vendored: `icons.js`, whose sprite draws the Google "G" and the MultipleCorp mark; `template/page-skeleton.html`, a template carrying demonstration content; `project-config.js`, the demonstration configuration; the other templates, documents, examples, tools and `serve.cmd`, which are not runtime; and the private lab, never.
5. **The server and the shell.** Every check of §11.1 and §11.2 passed and nothing was left unmeasured: the refused arguments, the methods, SP-4's headers, the path-traversal and Host refusals, the directory redirect, the font, the second start and the port's release; four screenshots at their exact sizes, seven same-origin requests per page load, no console message, no horizontal scroll and Inter loaded. Not measured, because they belong to S2-2's journey screens (SD-07): the side-by-side comparison, motion, focus, keyboard paths and target sizes.
6. **The phone test.** NOT RUN — the network profile is Public and the Owner chose not to change it; S2-2 or the Owner's preview carries it (SP-6). No firewall rule or network profile was changed.
7. **The vendor inventory.** Five scripts need the runtime API of the non-vendored `project-config.js`, and four the sprite of `icons.js`; their browser-storage keys conflict with SS-07; router.js fetches same-origin pages; the reference's demonstration content sits only in interactions.js's Login and Motion Study sections; 38 lines of components.css wait for the `mc-js` flag; the example-page blocks stay unused; the shell loads `InterVariable.woff2` only.
8. **The S2-1 inputs** ([S2-1 inputs](#stage-2--s2-1-inputs)), with the Owner's questions: a modified copy of `project-config.js` or an alternative (SP-2); the approval of the screen list (SD-01); how the simulated Google button of S2-J01 is drawn; a package for the verification tooling, only if needed (SD-07); later, the phone test's arrangement (SP-6). Before S2-1, Planning needs read access to the private prototype repository, which the Owner arranges.
9. **What was not done.** No S2-1 work or anything later; no journey page, panel, configuration file, override stylesheet, icon set or loaded script; no Stage 2 document beyond the drafts index; no edit of INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE, DESIGN_SYSTEM or ADMIN_FLOW and no change to a P0–P6 specification; no file of the reference and no binary in MULTIPLECORP; no DesainPakeAI command; nothing installed, upgraded or configured; no firewall, network-profile or GitHub setting changed; `main` not moved.
10. **After this.** Planning's independent verification of both repositories — the private one through the access the Owner arranges —, then the Owner's S2-1 authorization; nothing of S2-1 starts here.

## Stage 2 — S2-0 zero-context check

DIR-062 §14, run on 2026-10-13 by a fresh sub-agent of a read-only type, without write tools: it started at AGENTS.md and followed its reading order only, was told not to read source records 59–61, the prototype working copy, the reference's clone or this session, and was bound by DIR-046 §2. It read the working tree as it stood — S2-0's records uncommitted, before this section, gate S2-0 and TECH-031's hash table were written. Its verbatim report is kept outside both repositories, for this session only: 234 lines, 27,172 bytes, SHA-256 `8932a652ba201aa71aa6b1ba3006dfd6cefcdbc56ec2f2bb46082f3072e49ac7`. It states that it did not read the content of the local `refs/codex/` ref, and that its tool harness saved two large command outputs on its own, which it did not open.

| Question | Its answer, in brief | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| (a) The phase and Stage 2 | P7 IN_PROGRESS, the UX re-baseline; Stage 2 opened by the Owner through DIR-061, one run per authorization, in the order S2-0, S2-1, S2-2, the visual preview, S2-3, C2 with S2-4; S2-0 done under DIR-062, its closing steps still running in the working tree; S2-1 not authorized, needing Planning's verification and the Owner's authorization | Correct |

## Stage 2 — S2-0 acceptance and S2-1 authorization (DIR-063, DIR-064)

**DIR-063.** The Owner accepted S2-0 on 2026-10-13 as the checkpoint of the Stage 2 prototype preparation, on the executor's S2-0 report and Planning's independent verification of both repositories ([DIR-063](../../00-governance/DECISION_LOG.md#dir-063-dir-064-obs-024-and-tech-032--p7-ux-re-baseline-s2-0-acceptance-s2-1-authorization-baseline-and-stage-2-documents); the sixty-second source record). S2-0's results stand as this record's Stage 2 sections and Gate S2-0 record them.

**DIR-064's boundary.** Run S2-1 only (the sixty-third source record): the read-only verification of the baseline, the prototype repository and the tools; source records 62 and 63; the S2-1 documents under [stage2/](../stage2/README.md), non-normative drafts; the Owner's questions and the genuinely new gaps, nothing enacted; the narrow continuity records and the other records; the zero-context check, Gate S2-1, commit 10 and a normal push of `rebaseline/p7-ux`; then STOP before S2-2. The prototype repository is read only — nothing built there —; INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE, DESIGN_SYSTEM, ADMIN_FLOW and the P0–P6 specifications do not change, and no Stage 3 file is created.

## Stage 2 — Owner questions after S2-1

Nine questions, each OPEN, in full in [OWNER_QUESTIONS](../stage2/OWNER_QUESTIONS.md); none is enacted (DIR-064 §14.5), and the S2-1 documents use the design that fits the current rules.

| OQ | Question | Recommendation | Needed | Kind |

## Stage 2 — S2-1 report

The English equivalent of the report given to the Owner in Bahasa Indonesia at the stop (DIR-064 §20), without commit 10's SHA or push, which the commit cannot contain.

1. **The decision and the authorization.** DIR-063, the Owner's acceptance of S2-0, and DIR-064, the authorization of run S2-1 only, are recorded in the [decision log](../../00-governance/DECISION_LOG.md#dir-063-dir-064-obs-024-and-tech-032--p7-ux-re-baseline-s2-0-acceptance-s2-1-authorization-baseline-and-stage-2-documents) from source records 62 and 63, with the Owner's four answers in session.
2. **The baseline** ([S2-1 verification](#stage-2--s2-1-verification)): commit 9 `fb996c9ca0721329ca1b601c00398228603a533d` with its branch push verified, `main` at `a526daf`; the prototype repository unchanged at `503e83bd3ed720f2af1d7c5eb4bc0f84500b9edb`, only read.
3. **The documents** ([S2-1 documents](#stage-2--s2-1-documents)): nineteen non-normative drafts under `docs/07-ux-design/stage2/` — the page inventory (65 pages), the navigation map, the non-loss matrix (428 rows, none without a destination), the written flows (39 journeys and WFL-01–WFL-09), the authentication journeys, the localization architecture, the preview producer, the query-budget checks, the design-system mapping (424 of 424 tokens), the deviation register (GR-24–GR-31), the simulation register (SIM-01–SIM-16), the prototype plan with the screen list, the six journey specifications and OQ-01–OQ-09; every §11.4 count equal to Planning's.
4. **The six journeys:** 53 screens and the out-of-scope page; trace matrices of 13, 6, 11, 8, 16 and 10 steps — 64 in all; one step removed, S2-J02's row for projects past their deadline, which has no canonical signal (GAP-052); S2-R01–S2-R09 each specified with its scenario, trace rows and S2-2 check in S2-J05. Three read-only reviews of the six specifications led to corrections before completion, among them IP-08's confirmation for each received line (a new PS-35), the reservation limit answered by the server, one *Nota Terima Barang* per receiving event, `profit.view` for the Owner's project figures, and no "Catat Pengiriman" for an Admin without `delivery.record`.
5. **The probe:** run on 127.0.0.1 with the installed Chrome and Node.js built-ins, items a)–k) demonstrated; S2-2 needs no package, so no installation is put to the Owner.
6. **The continuity records:** AGENTS.md rule 10, EXECUTION_CONTEXT's visual-direction line and DESIGN_REFERENCES now state that the reference governs presentation only and stays non-normative until Stage 3 and APPR-011; this record's notes Z8 (GR-16 at the preview) and Z9 (the Stage 2 plan row), the SD-05 note on the non-loss matrix and the DIR-063 acceptance note; Z12 left as it is.
7. **Questions for the Owner:** OQ-01–OQ-09 with their recommendations and timing, in [Owner questions after S2-1](#stage-2--owner-questions-after-s2-1); new gaps GAP-054–GAP-057, each OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED.
8. **What S2-2 needs:** the Owner's answers to OQ-01, OQ-06, OQ-07 and OQ-09 — the screen list's approval among them — and the S2-2 authorization ([S2-2 inputs](#stage-2--s2-2-inputs)).
9. **Not done:** no prototype page built and nothing changed in the prototype repository; no normative P7 document and no P0–P6 specification changed; no reference clone made; no question enacted; S2-2 not started.

Its file list and hashes are in TECH-033.

## Stage 2 — GF-1 report

The English equivalent of the report given to the Owner in Bahasa Indonesia at the stop (DIR-068 §13), without commit 12's SHA or push, which the commit cannot contain.

1. **The baseline and the lab** ([GF-1 verification](#stage-2--gf-1-verification)): commit 11 `ba257169bfc9a3d9b2788f08644ecfbd848c5f65` and its push verified, `main` at `a526daf`; the prototype repository at `a273207e3ea7c906e311cecd567c00ff19cf98ad`, pushed by the Owner, private, 15 OK; Golden's public tag v1.0.0, its lists 41 OK and 55 OK; the lab — the folder Golden's freeze record names — 41 OK and 239 OK, only read; every item of §4 passing, and again at the resume of Part 3.

The classification of the 14 prototype pages: four Golden pages carry their content as static markup (01, 02, 03 and 00, with 05) and eight are module shells, each becoming an adapter frame with its module stylesheet direct and a replaced engine; PG-042 and PG-011 the largest; extending the pilot to the 53 screens estimated at about six to nine runs of GF-1's size.
4. **The deviations** ([DEVIATION_REGISTER §4](../stage2/DEVIATION_REGISTER.md#4-the-pilot-pages-gf-1); GOLDEN_FIRST_PILOT §5): kept, each with its reason — for accessibility GR-01, GR-04 as a rule, GR-05, GR-06, GR-07 and GR-24; for P0–P6 and repository rules GR-09, GR-14, GR-28, AGENTS.md rule 12, IP-08 and PS-27 without "Catatan"; for R1 and GD-03 GR-26 (a choice for the Owner), GR-27, GR-29, GR-11, GR-21, OC-05's order and GR-34's content; for the honesty elements and the Owner's decisions GR-33, GR-12, GR-32, GR-17 and GR-31 —; retired under K1 — GR-18 where Golden has the symbol, GR-25 with VP-8's no-looping rule, GR-34's layout and styling, and GR-22 and GR-35 where content still passes —; not exercised on the four pages — GR-03, GR-08, GR-10, GR-13, GR-15, GR-19, GR-20, GR-23 and GR-30 —; missing, found after the prototype commit — GR-02 and GR-16's variant B switch —; no new deviation.

11. **Instructions found in data**, treated as data and never followed: Golden's agent-directed files — the public tag's `AGENTS.md` and the lab's `docs/AGENT_START_HERE.md` — and the agent-facing notes in its documents; the load-order notes in the vendored scripts' headers, read as documentation; in the session, the continuation note after a context compaction, which offered the session's transcript for details — not read (DIR-046 §2).
12. **Working-rule notes:** the Owner paused the run during Part 3 and resumed it with "LANJUTKAN GF-1"; the resume verified §4 again, re-checked the last unit written and redid nothing finished. One write exceeded the working rules' about 8 KB: the evidence file `labels.json`, about 12 KB, written at once. After a context compaction the work continued from the repositories and the run's outputs; no transcript or session file was read.
13. **Not done:** the phone test (at the review); the Owner's review itself; GR-02 and GR-16's switch on the pilot pages, a second prototype commit not being authorized; the pilot's extension, S2-3, the usability kit, Checkpoint C2, S2-4 and Stages 3–5; OQ-02, OQ-03, OQ-04, OQ-05 and OQ-08 not enacted; no change to S2-2's 53 screens, a P0–P6 specification, INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE, DESIGN_SYSTEM or ADMIN_FLOW; nothing written in the lab and none of its tools run; no installation or configuration; `main` not moved.
14. **The guide for the Owner's review:** in the prototype's working copy `C:\Projects\multiplecorp-p7-prototype`, `node tools/serve.mjs` starts the read-only site server on 127.0.0.1:8780 — the pilot's start page `http://127.0.0.1:8780/pilot/` links the four pages and their states in Indonesian and English, PS-10 reached through the account menu and PS-11 through *Keluar* —, and `node tools/gf1-review/serve.mjs` the review page on `http://127.0.0.1:8790/`; Ctrl+C stops each. The review page's tabs: *Side by side* — each pair, the lab's original captioned "GOLDEN · lab original" and the pilot "PILOT · GF-1", in the modes side by side, swipe (a slider moves the boundary) and difference, with the pair's labels —; *Motion* — the Golden and pilot sequences of M1–M8, side by side, played at their real timing, at 0.25× or 0.1×, or frame by frame, with their animation records —; *320 px reflow*; *Deviations and open items*. On the pilot pages the marker "PROTOTIPE · data contoh" opens the panel, the account menu — on the phone *Lainnya* › *Akun* — switches the language, and *Masuk* has its own ID | EN switch. The lab can be opened read-only for the Owner's own look from its folder in the browser (file://) — its pages are only read; on file:// Golden's router falls back to full page loads, so the persistent shell's motion is judged on the review page. To judge: the likeness per page, the motion, the deviations retired and kept, GR-26's placement, GR-16's variant A or B — on S2-2's pages, where its switch exists —, the open items, and whether to extend the pilot. The phone test (SS-06): with the Owner present, on a trusted network the Owner sets to Private, `node tools/serve.mjs` started with `--host` set to the computer's private IPv4 address, the phone opening the pilot's start page at that address on port 8780; the server stopped afterwards (Ctrl+C), nothing left reachable.

## Stage 4 — reviews

*Written by run PC-3 under [DIR-080](../../00-governance/DECISION_LOG.md#dir-079-dir-080-obs-031-and-tech-038--p7-ux-re-baseline-c2-evidence-and-owner-decisions-run-pc-3-stage-3-completed-stage-4-and-gate-p7) SP-76, in English; with the next sections it records PC-3's Part 3. Every finding — the self-review's, WF-PC3's and the later reviews' — is a row below, fixed at Level 1 or an Owner question; none is dropped or recorded as passed.*

**The working severity scale.** C-2 of [DIR-053](../../00-governance/DECISION_LOG.md#dir-053-dir-054-obs-019-and-tech-027--p7-ux-re-baseline-decisions-stage-01-authorization-baseline-and-direction) governs a LOW non-material finding — it may be carried only with its disposition, owner, target phase and rationale — and no other scale is defined, so Stage 4 states its own, given to each reviewer word for word: **CRITICAL** — breaks a P0–P6 invariant or a security or authorization rule, loses a V1 capability, invents C2 evidence or records a PASS the Owner did not state, or would make P11 build something wrong in money, stock, company isolation or authentication; **HIGH** — a contract, requirement or record wrong or missing, so that P11 or the Owner at OG-2 would act on an incorrect statement, or a narrow amendment beyond its CA or CB item; **MEDIUM** — an inconsistency, ambiguity or gap between documents likely to cause rework or misreading, with one obviously correct reading; **LOW** — editorial, wording, formatting or a minor inconsistency, no effect on meaning. The authority levels are CHANGE_CONTROL's: 1 a correction within the authorized scope, 2 the Owner's decision, 3 an override of the Owner's intent.

**The reviews.**

1. **The self-review** of the completed drafts against SP-68's coverage and APPR-011's conditions: the coverage passing; CD-06's screens confirmed by the `DIR-079` marks; against `3b45ce8`, P0–P6 changed only in SP-72's files, the two contracts (SP-71) and the two anchors (SP-74). Two LOW findings, S4F-01 and S4F-02.
2. **WF-PC3 `pc3-stage4-review`**, the one workflow (DIR-080 §6), requested explicitly and started through the Owner's approval prompt: three agents in parallel, findings only, each prompt stating GF-2 §3.3's terms — A1, UX, usability and accessibility, 16 findings; A2, business, security and authorization fidelity, 17; A3, contracts, localization and the zero-context reading, 22 —; 55 findings, no blocker. The run's values: A1 528,300 tokens and 1,861,952 ms; A2 566,074 tokens and 2,183,192 ms; A3 624,153 tokens and 1,957,466 ms; the workflow 1,718,527 tokens, 340 tool calls, 2,190,402 ms. **After it, by script:** both repositories' `git status --porcelain --ignored`, HEAD, the reflog and the live remote refs identical to the snapshot taken before it (41 lines, SHA-256 `682e0d5dc80c138df8974cbdf0f619d11cd2dd2378b9ade26b4d14e63ed7a989`); no process left — the only new processes seven renderers of the Owner's own browser, children of its main process started from the desktop shell before the run, none headless or under remote debugging; no Node.js, Python, PHP or Java process. Every finding was then checked against the documents it cites; four duplicates merged — A3-19 into S4F-01, A1-02 into S4F-02, A3-12 into S4F-03, A3-17 into S4F-32.
3. **The final focused review** — one fresh read-only sub-agent, not part of WF-PC3 (DIR-080 §6, as GF-2 §12) —, after the fixes: it started at AGENTS.md, followed its reading order, and checked the fixes and gate P7's items, bound by the same terms. 17 findings — 1 CRITICAL, 3 HIGH, 8 MEDIUM, 5 LOW —: four new defects (S4F-54–S4F-57), twelve completions of earlier rows, each named in its row, and one Level 1 classification reconsidered (S4F-20, now OQ-25). The run's values: 709,789 tokens, 163 tool calls, 2,282,080 ms. **After it, by script:** both repositories identical to the snapshot taken before it, the uncommitted fixes included (59 lines, SHA-256 `b0e438f82189685bedf5e061937228c3236f20fc562eeee77f446bd8b3af9be8`); no process left by the run — the two new processes a renderer of the Owner's browser and a Python process of another Claude Code session working on another project, its script in that session's own folder. The reviewer disclosed one `git --version` it ran by mistake — it reads no repository, and the snapshot shows nothing changed — and the harness's own saving of three oversized tool outputs in the session's folder, outside both repositories; it wrote nothing else.
4. **The convergence review** — one fresh read-only sub-agent, the run's fifth agent, started under DIR-049's rule as the plan applies it to UX, the final review having found material findings, a correction of authorization among them (S4F-54), and bound by the same terms —. **Its first attempt** stalled before reporting; the harness stopped it after 600 s without progress, and it returned no report, so nothing of it is recorded as a finding or as a pass. **After it, by script:** both repositories identical to the snapshot taken before it (59 lines, SHA-256 `b0e438f82189685bedf5e061937228c3236f20fc562eeee77f446bd8b3af9be8`) and every file of the working tree identical in content (215 files hashed before and after it); its staging folder empty; no process left — the new processes those of the Owner's own Edge browser, restarted by its own startup under the Owner's profile, none headless or under remote debugging; no Node.js, Python, PHP or Java process. PC-3 paused there at the Owner's direction; at the resume the Owner approved its re-run once, as the same fifth agent, for its unfinished checks — all of them (DIR-080 §5; PC-3 verification). **The re-run** reviewed only the corrections made after the final review — a diff from the documents as that review read them, reconstructed by replaying the run's fix scripts on the Stage 3 commit and equal byte for byte to the Part 3 checkpoint once the later scripts ran, to `4b99b71`, 15 files —, in six checks: each final-review finding's disposition; authorization and security semantics against PERMISSIONS_MATRIX and SECURITY; interaction, business-visible and persistence semantics against P3–P6; the Owner questions; the records and counts; and any new CRITICAL, HIGH or Owner-level issue. It found the corrections resolving S4F-54 and the rest but ten points — 4 MEDIUM, 6 LOW, none CRITICAL, HIGH or Owner-level; no capability added or removed beyond P5; company scope, step-up and audit unchanged —: nine completions of earlier rows, each named in its row — S4F-20, S4F-29, S4F-36, S4F-38, S4F-39, S4F-44, S4F-46, S4F-56 and S4F-57 — and one defect older than the corrections that rewrote its rows (S4F-58); each was checked against the documents it cites and fixed at Level 1, one proposed text corrected — the dispatch's reversal is on PG-020 (INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE), not PG-024. The run's values: 651,900 tokens, 113 tool calls, 1,982,341 ms. **After it, by script:** both repositories identical to the snapshot taken before it, the Part 3 checkpoint included (41 lines, SHA-256 `05d4479f9a425fdcfcce6e645bee0b9cee807e8a35a975304dfc3f10c725345a`), and every file of the working tree identical in content (215 files hashed before and after it); its staging folder holding only the four inputs placed there, their SHA-256 unchanged; no process left — the two new processes renderers of the Owner's own Chrome browser, children of its main process started before the run, none headless or under remote debugging; no Node.js, Python, PHP or Java process. The reviewer disclosed one attempt to write its report in its staging folder, which the harness refused, so nothing was written; the harness's saving of one oversized search result in the session's folder, outside both repositories; and two early searches over `docs/` that returned lines of other source records, none of records 77 or 78; it ran no git or shell command and wrote nothing. **The convergence rule after it:** its corrections state in the contracts and records what the canonical documents already define — DATABASE §4.10's one DRAFT per chain, WF-DOC-01's draft, IP-19's own answer, J-FIN-08's and AX-31's entered amount, J-FUL-01's and WF-FIN-08's reason —; they change no authorization or security semantics, add no persistence or dependency mechanism and yield no HIGH or Owner-level finding, so DIR-049's rule as the plan applies it to UX calls for no further review — one would have needed a sixth agent, a STOP under DIR-080 §11 —; they were validated by script — SP-68's coverage and the links — and by reading against those documents.

The reports are kept verbatim outside both repositories, their lines, bytes and SHA-256 in TECH-038.

**The findings.** 58 rows — CRITICAL 3, HIGH 9, MEDIUM 36, LOW 10. 54 fixed at Level 1; 4 Owner questions, each with its recommendation for OG-2 and its Level 1 part applied: OQ-22 (S4F-03), OQ-25 (S4F-20, GAP-062), OQ-23 (S4F-29, GAP-061) and OQ-24 (S4F-39). 2. **Part 2** — Stage 3 completed (SP-71–SP-75), committed as `6f95c77b39f579a291cd8beaee2b131559226eee` (`docs: complete P7 Stage 3 documents (PC-3)`), pushed and verified: UF-01's document layout in DESIGN_SYSTEM D-DS-23 and on PG-014, PG-033, PG-036, PG-042 and PG-043 for a draft; CB-01–CB-03's narrow amendments of WORKFLOWS, DATABASE, CONCURRENCY_IDEMPOTENCY and PERFORMANCE, each marked `DIR-079`; LOCALIZATION's 306 owed keys rendered in English with the single validation pass; the two stale anchors; P9's answers.

The Owner paused the run inside Part 3 after the convergence review's first attempt stalled; its state was committed as the checkpoint `4b99b71ed47158962cde2517313ed65afacc240c` (`docs: apply P7 Stage 4 fixes (PC-3), part 1`), pushed and verified, after the resume's recovery check and §1.1's confirmation; the convergence review was then re-run once with the Owner's approval.
